Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in metabolism theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 396 and the invariant I(A,B) = 672 mod 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the metabolism space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 396 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 672. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The metabolism operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 696. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 40 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 696 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the metabolism structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 40. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
